Runs observation-to-POI aggregation (merge_obs.py) and JSON schema validation (validate_data.py). Always run both steps before considering data production complete. Always run this analysis before starting data production. ([epfl-timemachine/time-atlas-data-production](https://github.com/epfl-timemachine/time-atlas-data-production/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/timeatlas-pre-production-analysis))
